# Commands · C · part 5

1,472 entries, claude-oneshot to coauthor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **claude-oneshot**: non-interactive one-shot `claude -p` wrapper that writes stream-json output to a per-slug log file for downstream monitoring. Use this primitive from a parallel orchestrator (e.g. parallel-worktree v2) when each task needs an independent top-level Claude session with its own context budget instead of the cheaper Agent-tool fan-out. ([ThomasEdwardYorke/cc-triad-relay](https://github.com/ThomasEdwardYorke/cc-triad-relay/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness/commands/claude-oneshot.md) · ★1)

- **claudemd-analyze**: Read-only spec ↔ implementation coherence audit. Cross-references core ↔ extended §EXT refs, Sizing line accuracy (±20B), §0.1 headroom caps, MEMORY.md ↔ files bidirectional integrity, and §10 quick-check terms ↔ banned-vocab.patterns coverage. Findings graded CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW. Borrowed from github/spec-kit's /analyze pattern. ([sdsrss/claudemd](https://github.com/sdsrss/claudemd/blob/HEAD/commands/claudemd-analyze.md) · ★2)
- **claudemd-audit**: Aggregate claudemd rule-hits over the last N days. Shows top banned patterns, hook deny counts, per-spec-section heatmap, and bypass-escape-hatch usage. ([sdsrss/claudemd](https://github.com/sdsrss/claudemd/blob/HEAD/commands/claudemd-audit.md) · ★2)
- **claudemd-backup**: Backup and restore CLAUDE.md files — create backups before changes, list history, restore previous versions ([Gdm0714/claudemd-lint](https://github.com/Gdm0714/claudemd-lint/blob/HEAD/commands/claudemd-backup.md))
- **claudemd-bypass-audit**: R3 Step 2 — lesson-bypass detector. Joins memory-prompt-hint `suggest` events with subsequent transcript activity to compute cite-recall (applied / (applied + bypassed)) across recent sessions. Makes the §11 MEMORY.md read-the-file effectiveness observable. ([sdsrss/claudemd](https://github.com/sdsrss/claudemd/blob/HEAD/commands/claudemd-bypass-audit.md) · ★2)
- **claudemd-clean-residue**: Clean up leftover claudemd-sync-* / claudemd-memtags-hay-* sentinels and claudemd-(mockgh|work).* test sandbox dirs from $TMPDIR, stale tool-exhaust (session scratchpads, old fixtures) from ~/.claude/tmp per the spec §7-EXT retention window (mtime &gt; TMP_RETENTION_DAYS, default 7), and orphaned per-session sentinels from ~/.claude/.claudemd-state (ext-read-*, vocab-scan-*, failopen-*, mem-coverage-*). Default is dry-run; pass `--apply` to delete. ([sdsrss/claudemd](https://github.com/sdsrss/claudemd/blob/HEAD/commands/claudemd-clean-residue.md) · ★2)
- **claudemd-cleanup**: Review and restructure CLAUDE.md files to reduce context bloat through hierarchical organization ([rickym-h/jadefall-marketplace](https://github.com/rickym-h/jadefall-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-quality/commands/claudemd-cleanup.md) · ★1)
- **claudemd-cleanup**: Clean up stale and unused rules from CLAUDE.md — find rules not referenced in recent sessions and remove them ([Gdm0714/claudemd-lint](https://github.com/Gdm0714/claudemd-lint/blob/HEAD/commands/claudemd-cleanup.md))
- **claudemd-design-adopt**: Generate a thin, fact-based DESIGN.md from a UI project's real design-token sources and wire it into project CLAUDE.md. Use when (1) the user asks to configure design specs / 配置设计规范 / 设计规范 for agents, (2) UI sessions keep inventing colors or spacing instead of using the project's existing tokens, (3) a UI project (Vue/React/Svelte/Nuxt/Astro + a component lib or Tailwind/UnoCSS/SCSS) has design tokens but no DESIGN.md guiding agents. Modes - check (verify pointers), remove (unwire). Never invents design values - every generated line must trace to a detected file. ([sdsrss/claudemd](https://github.com/sdsrss/claudemd/blob/HEAD/commands/claudemd-design-adopt.md) · ★2)

- **click-argument-suppression**: Click injects one function parameter per CLI option. Commands with 5+ options inherently exceed CodeScene's "Excess Number of Function Arguments" threshold of 4. ([humansintheloop-dev/humansintheloop-dev-workflow-and-tools](https://github.com/humansintheloop-dev/humansintheloop-dev-workflow-and-tools/blob/HEAD/design-pattern-catalog/commands/click-argument-suppression.md) · ★44)

- **clipper**: Save online content as a clean, readable long-form piece ("clip") in the vault. Subcommands per platform — `x` (X / Twitter: video OR longform article, auto-detected from the URL) and `yt` (YouTube). The script does all the technical work (cookies, download, subtitle selection, VTT cleanup, audio-transcription fallback, or headless-browser article extraction) and hands you clean text; you only reframe a transcript into faithful prose, or save an article verbatim — never a summary. - **coach**: The maintenance skill for your Claude Code environment.
